### BC-0250 — WATCH
  - ✓ **underlying_job_or_problem**: EVIDENCED — Laptop fan controllers enforce fan presence checks and expect specific voltage/current ratings; reusing existing motherboard fan controller requires signal splitting to avoid BIOS errors about disconnected CPU fan; Laptop motherboards expect specific fan electrical characteristics (voltage, current) and will error if CPU fan signal is absent; users must either satisfy BIOS fan detection or disable monitoring entirely _(evidence: OBS-20260824-0065-b18035, OBS-20260825-0076-cdb8d6)_
  - ✓ **pain_severity**: EVIDENCED — SEVERE _(evidence: OBS-20260824-0065-b18035, OBS-20260825-0076-cdb8d6)_

  - ✓ **identifiable_buyer**: EVIDENCED — api_consumer _(evidence: OBS-20260824-0065-b18035)_
  - ✓ **current_workaround**: EVIDENCED — Direct connection between laptop fan controller (5V/0.5A rated) and laptop cooling fan; BIOS reads tachometer signal to verify fan presence and operation; Laptop motherboard BIOS firmware monitors fan connection; original 5V 0.5A fan controller header; user must manually wire PWM and tachometer signals separately from 12V power source _(evidence: OBS-20260824-0065-b18035, OBS-20260825-0076-cdb8d6)_
  - ✓ **why_solutions_fail**: EVIDENCED — Direct connection of 12V fan to 5V 0.5A-rated laptop header would either not power fan adequately or burn out motherboard port through overcurrent; boost converter approach fails due to insufficient current capacity and efficiency losses; Using boost converter from 5V motherboard port would burn out the port due to insufficient current rating (0.5A at 5V provides almost no power at 12V after conversion losses) _(evidence: OBS-20260824-0065-b18035, OBS-20260825-0076-cdb8d6)_
  - ✓ **potential_product_function**: EVIDENCED — Laptop fan controllers enforce fan presence checks and expect specific voltage/current ratings; reusing existing motherboard fan controller requires signal splitting to avoid BIOS errors about disconnected CPU fan; Laptop motherboards expect specific fan electrical characteristics (voltage, current) and will error if CPU fan signal is absent; users must either satisfy BIOS fan detection or disable monitoring entirely _(evidence: OBS-20260824-0065-b18035, OBS-20260825-0076-cdb8d6)_
  - · **willingness_to_pay**: INSUFFICIENT_DATA — None
